## Title screen v4 (2026-11-04, 0 credits) — CURRENT, use this
- She found v3 unusable (messy areas, broken font, crooked buttons). v4 = white bg + wooden vine board + official logo (unchanged, single line) + Mali-Bold 18 tagline + 2 hand-drawn pixel buttons (normal/hover/pressed).
- Board sampled from her reference at its true 7.5 px grid (no resampling), widened only by repeating plain columns. Script `art-src/title_v4/wide_board.py`.
- Assets `public/assets/ui/title/v4/` + `title_layout.json`. Doc `docs/title_screen_v4_2026-11-04.md`. Preview `docs/previews/title_v4_preview.gif`.
- Lesson: never downscale AI art by a non-integer factor; find its pixel grid and sample cell centres. Render Thai text at ≥18 px (antialiased mask thresholded at 50%), not fontmode '1' at small sizes.
- Board motion added: group bob ±2 px / 4 s + twinkles (`twinkle_4f.png`), see doc update section. Preview `docs/previews/title_v4_anim.gif`.
- Prompt for the code side not yet confirmed as sent.

## Wardrobe / character-creator idea (discussed 2026-11-04, NOT decided yet)
- She wants switchable outfits and to SELL outfits (shop / chest drops). This needs "way B": rebuild the girl as a LAYERED base (body/skin, hair, top, bottom, accessories) and redo all actions once on that base. Estimate about 70–100 credits; 3,242 credits remain, which reset on 2026-11-30.
- Free test already done: the current walk animation was recoloured by masks (cardigan to mint/pink, gingham pattern) and a straw hat and a bow were anchored to the head every frame. It works. Cleanup notes:
  - The eye and cardigan colours clash (both purple), so the new base must use non-overlapping colours per part.
  - The gingham pattern must follow the body when she walks.
- Cost tiers after the base exists:
  - colour/pattern swaps: free;
  - accessories (hats, clips, bows): 0–5 credits each;
  - new-shape outfits or hairstyles: about 30–80 credits each (their layer drawn over every frame).
- Adding NEW actions later costs extra for every shape-outfit and hairstyle, so lock the action list first.
- Character creator plan: skin, eye and hair colours by palette (free) + 3–4 hairstyles (the expensive part).
- Main outfit stays the purple cardigan + navy skirt. Her new design is a SPECIAL outfit (gingham pinafore dress + bucket hat with flower, colours A green / B pink / C blue / D purple), sold in the shop or dropped from chests. Make A once, then B–D are free colour swaps; the hat is a separate accessory. Idea: shop sells A/B, chests drop the rare C/D.

- **LOCKED action list for the layered girl (approved 2026-11-04).** Directions: S, E, N; W = mirror of E. Walk reduced from 8 to 4 directions.
  - Group 1 (must have):
    1. idle (breathe + blink)
    2. walk
    3. till
    4. water
    5. plant
    6. harvest
    7. cook at the campfire
    8. fishing (cast / wait / reel)
    9. eat
    10. bath at the pond

    11. pet Gio or pets (crouch)
    12. put food on the tray
    13. hold an item up (got item / open chest)
    14. nap sitting against the tree
    15. sleep / doze off on the spot
    16. faint
  - Group 3 (extra, later): happy jump, sad / homesick, run.
  - Estimate: groups 1+2 about 70–110 credits; group 3 adds about 15–25. Existing animations are used as motion references.
